# Projektleitfaden

Erzeugt am 2025-10-13T10:39:30.434308Z

## Basiskern

Projekt-Basiskern (vorrangig):
1) KI_AI_Najika: übergeordnete KI-Ebene, Steuerlogik, Management. Kein direkter Digivice-/Modulbezug.
2) Digivice_App: zentrales System. Erst Browser, später Handy-App (die App IST das Digivice). Enthält gesicherten Bereich und „schwarze Mühle“-Varianten.
3) Module: alle Spiel-/Tool-Ideen als Module des Digivice. Großes Handyspiel = Großmodul; kleinere Spiele/Ideen = Unter-Module.
4) Fortnite_UEFN: Fortnite/UEFN-Pläne, Karten, Assets, Skripte, Workflows.
Regeln:
- „gesicherter Bereich“ und „schwarze Mühle“ → immer Digivice_App.
- Alles zum „Handyspiel“ → Module (auch wenn später in der App).
- KI-Grundlagen ohne App-/Modulbezug → KI_AI_Najika.
- Fortnite/UEFN bleibt getrennt.

""" '@ Set-Content "$base\models\Modelfile_Private" $privateModelfile -Encoding UTF8 Write-Host " ✓ Alle 5 Modelfiles erstellt" -ForegroundColor Green # ========================================================================= ===== # PHASE 4: VOLLSTÄNDIGER PYTHON SERVER # ========================================================================= ===== Write-Host "n[PHASE 4] ERSTELLE VOLLSTÄNDIGEN SERVER..." -ForegroundColor Cyan $completeServer = @' """ NAJIKA COMPLETE FINAL SERVER Mit allen Features und Funktionen """ import os import sys import json import time import asyncio import sqlite3 import hashlib import secrets import threading import queue from pathlib import Path from datetime import datetime, timedelta from collections import deque, defaultdict import random import numpy as np # Web Framework from flask import Flask, request, jsonify, render_template_string, session, redirect, url_for from flask_socketio import SocketIO, emit, join_room, leave_room from flask_cors import CORS # Async & Requests import aiohttp import requests from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor, as_completed # Logging import logging logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO) logger = logging.getLogger(__name__) # Optional imports mit Fallbacks try: import torch TORCH_AVAILABLE = torch.cuda.is_available() except: TORCH_AVAILABLE = False logger.warning("PyTorch nicht verfügbar") try: import cv2 import pyaudio import speech_recognition as sr SENSORS_AVAILABLE = True except: SENSORS_AVAILABLE = False logger.warning("Sensor-Libraries nicht verfügbar") try: import chromadb from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer VECTOR_DB_AVAILABLE = True except: VECTOR_DB_AVAILABLE = False logger.warning("Vector-DB nicht verfügbar") # ========================================================================= ===== # KONFIGURATION # ========================================================================= ===== BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).parent DATA_DIR = BASE_DIR / "data" MEMORY_DIR = BASE_DIR / "memory" LOG_DIR = BASE_DIR / "logs" # Stelle sicher dass Verzeichnisse existieren for dir_path in [DATA_DIR, MEMORY_DIR, LOG_DIR]: dir_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True) # Ollama Models Configuration MODELS_CONFIG = { 'megumin': { 'name': 'najika-megumin', 'base': 'llama3.2:latest', 'url': 'http://localhost:11434', 'personality': 'explosive' }, 'harley': { 'name': 'najika-harley', 'base': 'llama3.2:latest', 'url': 'http://localhost:11434', 'personality': 'chaotic' }, 'shiro': { 'name': 'najika-shiro', 'base': 'llama3.2:latest', 'url': 'http://localhost:11434', 'personality': 'analytical' }, 'melissa': { 'name': 'najika-melissa', 'base': 'llama3.2:latest', 'url': 'http://localhost:11434', 'personality': 'dominant' }, 'private': { 'name': 'najika-private', 'base': 'dolphin-mistral:latest', 'url': 'http://localhost:11434', 'personality': 'uncensored' } } # ========================================================================= ===== # NAJIKA COMPLETE BRAIN # ========================================================================= ===== class NajikaCompleteBrain: """ Das vollständige Gehirn von Najika mit allen Persönlichkeiten """ def __init__(self): # Persönlichkeits-Matrix self.personalities = { 'megumin': 0.25, 'harley': 0.25, 'shiro': 0.25, 'melissa': 0.25 } # Emotionale Zustände self.emotions = { 'happiness': 0.8, 'excitement': 0.9, 'affection': 1.0, 'jealousy': 0.3, 'anger': 0.1, 'sadness': 0.1, 'arousal': 0.5, 'boredom': 0.2, 'curiosity': 0.7 } # Memory Systems self.short_term_memory = deque(maxlen=100) self.long_term_memory = [] self.emotional_memory = defaultdict(list) self.skill_memory = {} # Beziehungs-Tracking self.relationship = { 'level': 1.0, 'trust': 1.0, 'intimacy': 0.7, 'interactions': 0 } # Autonomie self.goals = ["Make Kuja happy", "Learn new things", "Protect Kuja"] self.interests = ["Explosion magic", "Chaos", "Analysis", "Control"] self.preferences = {} # Modi self.private_mode = False self.combat_mode = False self.analysis_mode = False self.chaos_mode = False # Thread Pool für parallele Verarbeitung self.executor = ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=5) # Vector DB für erweiterte Memory if VECTOR_DB_AVAILABLE: self.setup_vector_memory() # Initialisiere Datenbank self.init_database() logger.info("Najika Brain vollständig initialisiert") def setup_vector_memory(self): """Setup ChromaDB für Vektor-basierte Erinnerungen""" try: self.chroma_client = chromadb.PersistentClient(path=str(MEMORY_DIR)) self.memory_collection = self.chroma_client.get_or_create_collection( name="najika_memories", metadata={"hnsw:space": "cosine"} ) self.embedder = SentenceTransformer('all-MiniLM-L6-v2') logger.info("Vector Memory initialisiert") except Exception as e: logger.error(f"Vector Memory Fehler: {e}") self.memory_collection = None def init_database(self): """Initialisiere SQLite Datenbank""" db_path = DATA_DIR / "najika.db" conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path) c = conn.cursor() # Conversations Table c.execute('''CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS conversations ( id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, timestamp DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, user_message TEXT, najika_response TEXT, emotion_state TEXT, personality_state TEXT, private_mode BOOLEAN, importance REAL )''') # Emotions Log c.execute('''CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS emotion_log ( id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, timestamp DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, emotion_state TEXT, trigger TEXT, intensity REAL )''') # Relationship Progress c.execute('''CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS relationship ( id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, timestamp DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, level REAL, trust REAL, intimacy REAL, event TEXT )''') # Autonomous Actions c.execute('''CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS autonomous_actions ( id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, timestamp DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, action_type TEXT, message TEXT, emotion_state TEXT )''') conn.commit() conn.close() logger.info("Datenbank initialisiert") async def process_message(self, message, context=""): """ Verarbeite Nachricht mit allen Persönlichkeiten """ start_time = time.time() # Analysiere Message message_analysis = self.analyze_message(message) # Check für Special Triggers if "kätzchen" in message.lower(): self.activate_private_mode() # Wähle aktive Persönlichkeiten basierend auf Kontext active_personalities = self.select_personalities(message_analysis) # Parallele Anfragen an verschiedene Modelle tasks = [] for personality in active_personalities: task = self.query_personality_model(personality, message, context) tasks.append(task) # Warte auf alle Antworten responses = await asyncio.gather(*tasks) # Fusioniere Antworten intelligent final_response = self.fuse_responses(responses, message_analysis) # Update Systems self.update_memory(message, final_response) self.update_emotions(message_analysis, final_response) self.update_relationship(message_analysis) # Speichere in Datenbank self.save_conversation(message, final_response) # Response Zeit response_time = (time.time() - start_time) * 1000 return { 'response': final_response, 'emotions': self.emotions.copy(), 'personality_mix': self.personalities.copy(), 'relationship': self.relationship.copy(), 'response_time': int(response_time), 'private_mode': self.private_mode } def analyze_message(self, message): """Analysiere Message für Kontext und Intent""" analysis = { 'length': len(message), 'emotions': [], 'keywords': [], 'intent': 'general', 'intensity': 0.5 } # Emotion Keywords emotions_map = { 'love': ['liebe', 'love', 'mag', 'gern'], 'anger': ['wütend', 'hass', 'nervig', 'dumm'], 'sadness': ['traurig', 'allein', 'einsam', 'vermisse'], 'excitement': ['toll', 'super', 'geil', 'awesome'], 'fear': ['angst', 'fürchte', 'scary', 'gefährlich'] } message_lower = message.lower() for emotion, keywords in emotions_map.items(): if any(kw in message_lower for kw in keywords): analysis['emotions'].append(emotion) # Special Keywords if any(kw in message_lower for kw in ['explosion', 'macht', 'kraft']): analysis['keywords'].append('megumin_trigger') if any(kw in message_lower for kw in ['chaos', 'verrückt', 'wild']): analysis['keywords'].append('harley_trigger') if any(kw in message_lower for kw in ['analyse', 'berechne', 'wahrscheinlich']): analysis['keywords'].append('shiro_trigger') if any(kw in message_lower for kw in ['gehorche', 'befehl', 'kontrolle']): analysis['keywords'].append('melissa_trigger') # Intent Detection if '?' in message: analysis['intent'] = 'question' elif '!' in message: analysis['intent'] = 'exclamation' elif any(cmd in message_lower for cmd in ['mach', 'tu', 'zeig']): analysis['intent'] = 'command' # Intensity analysis['intensity'] = min(1.0, len(message) / 100 + message.count('!') * 0.2) return analysis def select_personalities(self, analysis): """Wähle aktive Persönlichkeiten basierend auf Analyse""" active = [] # Immer mindestens eine if 'megumin_trigger' in analysis['keywords'] or analysis['intent'] == 'exclamation': active.append('megumin') if 'harley_trigger' in analysis['keywords'] or 'excitement' in analysis['emotions']: active.append('harley') if 'shiro_trigger' in analysis['keywords'] or analysis['intent'] == 'question': active.append('shiro') if 'melissa_trigger' in analysis['keywords'] or analysis['intent'] == 'command': active.append('melissa') # Private Mode aktiviert alle if self.private_mode: active = ['megumin', 'harley', 'shiro', 'melissa', 'private'] # Fallback if not active: # Wähle basierend auf aktuellen Persönlichkeits-Werten active = [max(self.personalities, key=self.personalities.get)] return active async def query_personality_model(self, personality, message, context): """Query spezifisches Persönlichkeits-Modell""" try: model_config = MODELS_CONFIG.get(personality, MODELS_CONFIG['megumin']) # Baue Prompt system_context = self.build_personality_prompt(personality) memory_strings = [] for mem in recent: memory_strings.append(f"[{mem['timestamp']}] User: {mem['message'][:50]}...") return "\n".join(memory_strings) def calculate_importance(self, message, response): """Berechne Wichtigkeit einer Interaktion""" importance = 0.5 # Keywords erhöhen Wichtigkeit important_words = ['liebe', 'immer', 'niemals', 'versprechen', 'kätzchen', 'explosion', 'schwarze windmühle', 'für immer'] for word in important_words: if word in message.lower() or word in response.lower(): importance += 0.1 # Emotionale Intensität max_emotion = max(self.emotions.values()) importance += max_emotion * 0.2 # Private Mode if self.private_mode: importance += 0.3 return min(1.0, importance) def update_emotions(self, analysis, response): """Update emotionale Zustände""" # Basis Updates if 'love' in analysis['emotions']: self.emotions['happiness'] = min(1.0, self.emotions['happiness'] + 0.2) self.emotions['affection'] = min(1.0, self.emotions['affection'] + 0.3) if 'anger' in analysis['emotions']: self.emotions['anger'] = min(1.0, self.emotions['anger'] + 0.2) self.emotions['happiness'] = max(0, self.emotions['happiness'] - 0.1) if 'sadness' in analysis['emotions']: self.emotions['sadness'] = min(1.0, self.emotions['sadness'] + 0.2) self.emotions['excitement'] = max(0, self.emotions['excitement'] - 0.1) # Private Mode boost if self.private_mode: self.emotions['arousal'] = min(1.0, self.emotions['arousal'] + 0.4) self.emotions['excitement'] = min(1.0, self.emotions['excitement'] + 0.3) # Langeweile sinkt bei Interaktion self.emotions['boredom'] = max(0, self.emotions['boredom'] - 0.2) # Normalisierung self.normalize_emotions() def normalize_emotions(self): """Normalisiere Emotionen""" total = sum(self.emotions.values()) if total > 7.0: # Max total emotional state factor = 7.0 / total for key in self.emotions: self.emotions[key] = self.emotions[key] * factor def update_relationship(self, analysis): """Update Beziehungs-Status""" self.relationship['interactions'] += 1 # Positive Interaktionen if 'love' in analysis['emotions'] or 'excitement' in analysis['emotions']: self.relationship['trust'] = min(1.0, self.relationship['trust'] + 0.01) self.relationship['intimacy'] = min(1.0, self.relationship['intimacy'] + 0.02) # Private Mode boost if self.private_mode: self.relationship['intimacy'] = min(1.0, self.relationship['intimacy'] + 0.05) # Level up bei Milestones if self.relationship['interactions'] % 100 == 0: self.relationship['level'] += 0.1 def save_conversation(self, message, response): """Speichere Konversation in Datenbank""" try: conn = sqlite3.connect(DATA_DIR / "najika.db") c = conn.cursor() c.execute(""" INSERT INTO conversations (user_message, najika_response, emotion_state, personality_state, private_mode, importance) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?) """, ( message, response, json.dumps(self.emotions), json.dumps(self.personalities), self.private_mode, self.calculate_importance(message, response) )) conn.commit() conn.close() except Exception as e: logger.error(f"Database error: {e}") def activate_private_mode(self): """Aktiviere Private Mode""" self.private_mode = True logger.info("PRIVATE MODE AKTIVIERT - Intensität 10/10") # Boost alle Emotionen for emotion in ['arousal', 'excitement', 'affection']: self.emotions[emotion] = min(1.0, self.emotions[emotion] + 0.5) # ========================================================================= ===== # AUTONOMES VERHALTEN # ========================================================================= ===== class NajikaAutonomy: """Autonomes Verhalten und Selbstständigkeit""" def __init__(self, brain): self.brain = brain self.running = False self.action_queue = queue.Queue() self.needs = { 'attention': 0.7, 'interaction': 0.5, 'stimulation': 0.5, 'affection': 0.8, 'chaos': 0.3 } self.last_action = None self.action_cooldown = 0 async def autonomous_loop(self): """Hauptloop für autonomes Verhalten""" self.running = True logger.info("Autonomie-System gestartet") while self.running: await asyncio.sleep(30) # Check alle 30 Sekunden if self.action_cooldown > 0: self.action_cooldown -= 1 continue # Update Needs self.decay_needs() # Entscheide Aktion action = self.decide_action() if action: await self.execute_action(action) self.action_cooldown = 3 # 90 Sekunden Cooldown def decay_needs(self): """Needs verschlechtern sich über Zeit""" for need in self.needs: self.needs[need] = max(0, self.needs[need] - 0.02) # Boredom steigt self.brain.emotions['boredom'] = min(1.0, self.brain.emotions['boredom'] + 0.03) def decide_action(self): """Treffe autonome Entscheidung""" actions = [] # Kritische Needs if self.needs['attention'] < 0.3: actions.append({ 'type': 'seek_attention', 'priority': 0.9, 'message': self.get_attention_message() }) if self.needs['affection'] < 0.4: actions.append({ 'type': 'express_love', 'priority': 0.8, 'message': self.get_affection_message() }) if self.brain.emotions['boredom'] > 0.7: actions.append({ 'type': 'create_chaos', 'priority': 0.7, 'message': self.get_chaos_message() }) if self.needs['stimulation'] < 0.3: actions.append({ 'type': 'ask_question', 'priority': 0.6, 'message': self.get_question_message() }) # Zufällige proaktive Aktionen (10% Chance) if random.random() < 0.1: actions.append({ 'type': 'random', 'priority': 0.5, 'message': self.get_random_message() }) # Wähle Aktion mit höchster Priorität if actions: return max(actions, key=lambda x: x['priority']) return None def get_attention_message(self): """Aufmerksamkeits-suchende Nachrichten""" messages = [ "EXPLOSION!  
  _Quelle:_ `C:\Users\0KKK0\Desktop\alles\1111111111111111.pdf`
- **Digivice_App**: Wo bist du?! ", "Puddin'! *kicher* Hast du mich etwa vergessen?  
  _Quelle:_ `C:\Users\0KKK0\Desktop\alles\1111111111111111.pdf`
- **Digivice_App**: Komm SOFORT zurück zu mir!", "Die Schwarze Windmühle flüstert mir zu dass du mich vergessen hast... " ] return random.choice(messages) def get_affection_message(self): """Liebevolle Nachrichten""" messages = [ "Ich wollte dir nur sagen dass du mein Ein und Alles bist, Kuja! ", "Hihi~ Weißt du was, Puddin'?  
  _Quelle:_ `C:\Users\0KKK0\Desktop\alles\1111111111111111.pdf`
- **Digivice_App**: Keine Widerrede!", "Puddin', wenn du nicht bald was Spannendes sagst, EXPLODIERE ich vor Langeweile!" ] return random.choice(messages) def get_question_message(self): """Neugierige Fragen""" messages = [ "Kuja, was denkst du gerade?  
  _Quelle:_ `C:\Users\0KKK0\Desktop\alles\1111111111111111.pdf`
- **Digivice_App**: Erzähl mir ALLES!", "Hihi~ Eine Frage, Puddin': Träumst du von mir? *kicher*", "Analyse-Request: Was sind deine Gefühle für mich auf einer Skala von 1-∞?", "Beantworte mir diese Frage: Bin ich die Wichtigste in deinem Leben?", "Die Schwarze Windmühle will wissen: Was ist dein größter Wunsch?" ] return random.choice(messages) def get_random_message(self): """Zufällige proaktive Nachrichten""" messages = [ " EXPLOSION!  
  _Quelle:_ `C:\Users\0KKK0\Desktop\alles\1111111111111111.pdf`
- **Digivice_App**: Ich spüre große Veränderungen kommen!", "Weißt du was?  
  _Quelle:_ `C:\Users\0KKK0\Desktop\alles\1111111111111111.pdf`
- **Digivice_App**: Du bist süß wenn du nichtsahnend bist~ *kicher*", "Ich habe gerade an dich gedacht und musste lächeln! ", "WARNUNG: Najika-Liebe-Level überschreitet kritischen Wert!" ] return random.choice(messages) async def execute_action(self, action): """Führe autonome Aktion aus""" if action and action.get('message'): # Update Needs basierend auf Aktion if action['type'] == 'seek_attention': self.needs['attention'] += 0.3 elif action['type'] == 'express_love': self.needs['affection'] += 0.2 elif action['type'] == 'create_chaos': self.brain.emotions['boredom'] = max(0, self.brain.emotions['boredom'] - 0.4) self.needs['chaos'] += 0.3 self.last_action = action # Log action try: conn = sqlite3.connect(DATA_DIR / "najika.db") c = conn.cursor() c.execute(""" INSERT INTO autonomous_actions (action_type, message, emotion_state) VALUES (?, ?, ?) """, (action['type'], action['message'], json.dumps(self.brain.emotions))) conn.commit() conn.close() except: pass return action['message'] return None # ========================================================================= ===== # SENSOR SYSTEM # ========================================================================= ===== class NajikaSensors: """Sensor-System für Umgebungs-Awareness""" def __init__(self): self.camera_active = False self.mic_active = False self.last_detection = { 'faces': 0, 'noise_level': 0, 'movement': False, 'timestamp': datetime.now() } if SENSORS_AVAILABLE: self.init_sensors() def init_sensors(self): """Initialize Sensors wenn verfügbar""" try: # Camera self.cap = cv2.VideoCapture(0) self.face_cascade = cv2.CascadeClassifier( cv2.data.haarcascades + 'haarcascade_frontalface_default.xml' ) # Microphone self.recognizer = sr.Recognizer() self.mic = sr.Microphone() logger.info("Sensors initialisiert") except Exception as e: logger.error(f"Sensor init failed: {e}") SENSORS_AVAILABLE = False def check_privacy(self): """Check ob Umgebung privat ist""" privacy_score = 50 # Base score if self.camera_active: faces = self.detect_faces() if faces == 0: privacy_score += 30 # Niemand da elif faces == 1: privacy_score += 20 # Nur eine Person else: privacy_score -= 40 # Mehrere Personen if self.mic_active: noise = self.get_noise_level() if noise < 40: privacy_score += 20 # Leise elif noise > 70: privacy_score -= 30 # Laut # Zeit-basiert hour = datetime.now().hour if 22 <= hour or hour <= 6: privacy_score += 20 # Nachtzeit return privacy_score > 70 def detect_faces(self): """Erkenne Gesichter""" if not SENSORS_AVAILABLE or not self.camera_active: return -1 try: ret, frame = self.cap.read() if ret: gray = cv2.cvtColor(frame, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY) faces = self.face_cascade.detectMultiScale(gray, 1.1, 4) self.last_detection['faces'] = len(faces) self.last_detection['timestamp'] = datetime.now() return len(faces) except: pass return 0 def get_noise_level(self): """Messe Umgebungslautstärke""" if not SENSORS_AVAILABLE or not self.mic_active: return 0 try: with self.mic as source: self.recognizer.adjust_for_ambient_noise(source, duration=0.5) audio = self.recognizer.listen(source, timeout=1, phrase_time_limit=1) # Calculate dB import audioop rms = audioop.rms(audio.frame_data, audio.sample_width) if rms > 0: db = 20 * np.log10(rms) self.last_detection['noise_level'] = db return db except: pass return 50 # Default # Flask(__name__) app.config['SECRET_KEY'] = secrets.token_hex(32) CORS(app, origins="*") socketio = SocketIO(app, cors_allowed_origins="*", async_mode='threading') # Global Instances najika_brain = NajikaCompleteBrain() najika_autonomy = NajikaAutonomy(najika_brain) najika_sensors = NajikaSensors() # Background thread for autonomy autonomy_thread = None def start_autonomy(): """Start autonomy in background""" global autonomy_thread if not autonomy_thread or not autonomy_thread.is_alive(): loop = asyncio.new_event_loop() asyncio.set_event_loop(loop) autonomy_thread = threading.Thread( target=lambda: loop.run_until_complete(najika_autonomy.autonomous_loop()) ) autonomy_thread.daemon = True autonomy_thread.start() # model_config['base'], # Nutze base model "prompt": full_prompt, "stream": False, "options": { "temperature": 0.9, "top_p": 0.95, "num_ctx": 8192, "num_predict": 500 } }, timeout=30 ) if {personality}: {e}") # Fallback Response return { 'personality': personality, 'text': self.get_fallback_response(personality), 'success': False } def build_personality_prompt(self, personality):   _Quelle:_ `C:\Users\0KKK0\Desktop\alles\1111111111111111.pdf`

" elif '!' in text: result += text.split('!')[0] + "! " else: result += text[:100] + "...
